# Licences

notekpr is free software: you may use, share and change it under the GNU Affero General Public License, version 3 or later (AGPL-3.0-or-later), whose text is the [LICENSE](https://github.com/coolapso/notekpr/blob/main/LICENSE) file that comes with it. Its source is at [github.com/coolapso/notekpr](https://github.com/coolapso/notekpr).

It is also made of other people's work, under licences of their own that ask for their notices to go with every copy. Here they are, for all of notekpr's programs together: the server, the web app, the CLI and the desktop app (the server's image and the desktop app have the web app inside). Every one of them comes with this list: the web app as its *Licences* page (Settings → Account), the server's image as `/usr/share/notekpr/THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`, the packages and installers as a `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES` file next to notekpr's own licence.

`scripts/licenses.py` writes it from the crates' own files: change that, not this.

## Rust's standard library

Every program has Rust's standard library in it (`std`, `core`, `alloc`), under MIT OR Apache-2.0, here under MIT:

```

It is also built from crates of its own, listed with the others below (Rust 1.99.1's, the Rust notekpr's releases are built with), and its `compiler_builtins` has parts of LLVM's compiler-rt and of musl's libm in it:

## In notekpr's own code

### Drawings

Drawings port parts of rough.js, perfect-freehand, Excalidraw and other libraries (MIT), and of fdlibm and V8. In the server, web app, CLI and desktop app.

`crates/draw/PORTED.md`:

```
# Code this crate ports

`notekpr-draw` is written in Rust, but parts of it are ports (translations)
of JavaScript libraries, so that drawings look exactly like they do in
Excalidraw. Those libraries are under the MIT license, whose notice follows; the
fractional indices (`order.rs`) follow fractional-indexing, which is CC0,
and Obsidian's drawing files (`obsidian.rs`) are read and written as the
obsidian-excalidraw-plugin (Zsolt Viczian) does, which is under the
AGPL-3.0 like notekpr.
No JavaScript is used or shipped.

So that numbers come out exactly as in a browser, `src/js/math.rs` also
ports fdlibm's `sin`, `cos`, `tan`, `acos`, `asin`, `atan` and `atan2`
the way V8 has them, and `src/js.rs` computes `Math.hypot` the way V8 does; their
notices are at the end.

| What | Ported from | Copyright |
|---|---|---|
| hand-drawn shapes | rough.js 4.6.4 | (c) 2019 Preet Shihn |
| hachure fills | hachure-fill 0.5.2 | (c) 2023 Preet Shihn |
| curves to points | points-on-curve 0.2.0 / 1.0.1 | (c) 2020 Preet Shihn |
| SVG paths to points | points-on-path 0.2.1 | (c) 2020 Preet |
| SVG path data | path-data-parser 0.1.0 | (c) 2020 Preet Shihn |
| pen strokes | perfect-freehand 1.2.0 | (c) 2021 Stephen Ruiz Ltd |
| element shapes, file formats, constant-width strokes (laser-pointer), the editor (`src/edit/`: selecting, transforming, labels, binding, the line editor) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| the grid, snapping to other elements (`snapping.ts`), and how the web app draws them (`staticScene.ts`, `renderSnaps.ts`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| elbow arrows' routing (A* around the shapes they connect) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| sticky notes: their paper, date and fitted text (`sticky.rs`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| frames: what is in them, their names (`edit/frame.rs`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| the library: its items, what can't go in, duplicates (`library.rs`, `edit/library.rs`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| several library items put in at once: each copied apart, then laid out on a square grid (`library.rs`, `edit/library.rs`: `data/library.ts`'s `distributeLibraryItemsOnSquareGrid` and its test, `duplicateElements` per item) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| the web app's saved shapes panel: picking shapes (Shift-click ranges, `onItemSelectToggle`), putting in or dragging those picked, exporting and removing just them, Escape letting them go (`crates/web/src/views/library.rs`: `LibraryMenu.tsx`, `LibraryMenuItems.tsx`, `LibraryUnit.tsx`, `LibraryMenuHeaderContent.tsx`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| the drawing inside PNG and SVG pictures of it (`embed.rs`: `data/image.ts`, `data/encode.ts`, `scene/export.ts`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| what a picture of a drawing shows: the selection, a frame exported alone (`export.rs`, `svg.rs`: `data/index.ts`'s `prepareElementsForExport`, `scene/export.ts`'s `exportingFrame`, `frame.ts`'s `getElementsOverlappingFrame`) | Excalidraw | (c) 2020 Excalidraw |
| compression of Obsidian's drawings | lz-string 1.5 | (c) 2013 pieroxy |
| `Math.sin`, `Math.cos`, `Math.tan`, `Math.acos`, `Math.asin`, `Math.atan`, `Math.atan2` | fdlibm 5.3, as in V8 | (c) 1993 Sun Microsystems; the V8 project authors |
| `Math.hypot` | V8 | the V8 project authors |

The test fixture `tests/fixtures/perfect-freehand.json` holds
perfect-freehand's own test inputs and snapshots, from its repository
(MIT, as above). `tests/fixtures/smiley_embedded_v2.png`, `.svg` and
`test_embedded_v1.png`, `.svg` are pictures Excalidraw exported with the
drawing inside, from its repository's test fixtures (MIT, as above).

### Chemistry

### Fonts

The fonts drawings and math are written in, under the SIL Open Font License 1.1 and MIT. In the web app.

`crates/web/static/fonts/LICENSES.md`:

```
# The fonts of drawings

The fonts drawings are written in, as Excalidraw ships them (split by
character ranges, so a browser downloads only what a drawing uses). notekpr
serves them itself: drawings never load anything from elsewhere.
`drawing-fonts.css` declares them.

| Font | Folder | By | License |
|---|---|---|---|
| Excalifont | `Excalifont/` | Ján Filípek / DizajnDesign, based on Virgil | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Virgil | `Virgil/` | Your Own Font Foundry, for Excalidraw | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Nunito | `Nunito/` | Vernon Adams, Cyreal | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Lilita One | `Lilita/` | Juan Montoreano | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Comic Shanns | `ComicShanns/` | Shannon Miwa, Jesus Gonzalez | MIT |
| Cascadia Code | `Cascadia/` | Microsoft | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Liberation Sans | `Liberation/` | Red Hat | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |
| Assistant | `Assistant/` | Ben Nathan | SIL Open Font License 1.1 |

Not shipped: Xiaolai (Excalidraw's handwriting for Chinese and Japanese,
13 MB); such text falls back to the system's fonts.

## The math font

Math (LaTeX in notes) is typeset by the browser as MathML, in Noto Sans
Math (Google, SIL Open Font License 1.1), `NotoSansMath/`. It is the copy
math-core ships (`playground/fonts/NotoSansMath-Regular-centered-diacritics-primes.woff2`
at its tag v0.9.0, from [math-core-fonts](https://github.com/tmke8/math-core-fonts)):
the same glyphs, with primes and three accents placed so that Chromium and
Safari draw them right. `style.css` declares it; a browser downloads it the
first time a note shows math.

The SIL Open Font License 1.1 is in `OFL.txt`; Comic Shanns' MIT license in
`ComicShanns/LICENSE.txt`.
```

Copies notekpr changed, whose source is in notekpr's: openidconnect 4.0.1 ([vendor/openidconnect](https://github.com/coolapso/notekpr/tree/main/vendor/openidconnect)), sqlx-sqlite 0.9.0 ([vendor/sqlx-sqlite](https://github.com/coolapso/notekpr/tree/main/vendor/sqlx-sqlite)), yrs 0.28.0 ([vendor/yrs](https://github.com/coolapso/notekpr/tree/main/vendor/yrs)).

## Source of the MPL-2.0 crates

The Mozilla Public License 2.0 asks that its files' source be available. notekpr has these crates as they are published on crates.io, unchanged; their source is there: [cssparser 0.37.0](https://crates.io/crates/cssparser/0.37.0), [cssparser 0.38.0](https://crates.io/crates/cssparser/0.38.0), [cssparser-macros 0.7.1](https://crates.io/crates/cssparser-macros/0.7.1), [dtoa-short 0.3.5](https://crates.io/crates/dtoa-short/0.3.5), [option-ext 0.2.0](https://crates.io/crates/option-ext/0.2.0), [selectors 0.38.0](https://crates.io/crates/selectors/0.38.0).

## Licence texts

Each crate's licence and notice files, as it ships them. Of a choice of licences (MIT OR Apache-2.0, say), notekpr takes MIT where it can, and only that one's text is here.

```
# Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/dugong`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

## Dagre

Dugong is a modified Rust translation of Dagre's directed graph layout pipeline.

- Version: `2.0.2`
- Source: https://github.com/dagrejs/dagre.git @ `ba986662394f8f3ed608717194e5958f3386ce01`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/dagre/LICENSE`
```

# Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/dugong-graphlib`.

**manatee 0.10.0-alpha.2** (THIRD\_PARTY\_NOTICES.md):

```
# Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/manatee`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

## cose-base 1.x

Manatee contains Rust translations and adaptations of CoSE layout behavior from this baseline.

- Version: `1.0.3`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/cose-base.git @ `914bfe712991534af1d8b795d6f262687edc2563`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/cose-base-v1/LICENSE`

## cose-base 2.x

Manatee contains Rust translations and adaptations of the newer CoSE base behavior from this baseline.

- Version: `2.2.0`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/cose-base.git @ `37f07ed2b8803211ec6c74110574cc47c156a136`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/cose-base-v2/LICENSE`

## Cytoscape.js

Architecture layout and styling use source-backed Cytoscape behavior and defaults.

- Version: `3.34.4`
- Source: https://github.com/cytoscape/cytoscape.js.git @ `e394aa858b0699a67be8f9ab426823ae1aae7813`
- Relationship: `behavior-reference`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/cytoscape/LICENSE`

## cytoscape.js-cose-bilkent

Manatee includes source-backed CoSE-Bilkent layout behavior translated to Rust.

- Version: `4.1.0`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/cytoscape.js-cose-bilkent.git @ `999090a8438b4f14788d636ef4fd7a5355e29e8c`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/cytoscape-cose-bilkent/LICENSE`

## cytoscape.js-fcose

The headless Architecture layout is a modified Rust implementation of FCoSE behavior.

- Version: `2.2.0`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/cytoscape.js-fcose.git @ `78afcf96512a409abc903699277ad616c02dfad9`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/cytoscape-fcose/LICENSE`

## layout-base 1.x

Manatee translates shared layout-base geometry, graph, and force-layout primitives from this baseline.

- Version: `1.0.2`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/layout-base.git @ `836898aa4a88e2794774997d7128b383108a3d5a`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/layout-base-v1/LICENSE`

## layout-base 2.x

Manatee also follows the newer layout-base behavior selected by the FCoSE dependency graph.

- Version: `2.0.1`
- Source: https://github.com/iVis-at-Bilkent/layout-base.git @ `3f7549940feef31416cc35ef8256282ebc4d1ecd`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/layout-base-v2/LICENSE`
```

**merman-render 0.10.0-alpha.2** (THIRD\_PARTY\_NOTICES.md):

```
# Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/merman-render`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

## d3-path

The Pie SVG renderer translates D3 path rounding and arc serialization, including the 3.1.1 correction for rounded coincident endpoints of large arcs.

- Version: `3.1.1`
- Source: https://github.com/d3/d3-path.git @ `b9ab34592535861e44ce9db03de4db2c2c9c866a`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `ISC`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/d3-path/LICENSE`

## d3-shape

The SVG parity layer translates D3 curve algorithms, including basis, natural, step, cardinal, bump, and Catmull-Rom variants. The current reference graph uses d3-shape 3.2.1, whose curve source is unchanged from this translation baseline.

- Version: `3.2.0`
- Source: https://github.com/d3/d3-shape.git @ `8ec82658454750cfa29efb1e0ea514e3dd9b2297`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `ISC`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/d3-shape/LICENSE`

## fmin

The Venn layout kernel translates the fmin Nelder-Mead and conjugate-gradient optimization behavior.

- Version: `0.0.4`
- Source: https://github.com/benfred/fmin.git @ `6b155c9f4a6ecf73ea5d71666da8e5dcd418b18b`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `BSD-3-Clause`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/fmin/LICENSE`

## Mermaid

Merman independently implements Mermaid-compatible behavior while translating selected algorithms, generating defaults, copying architecture icon data, and retaining upstream fixtures and snapshots.

- Version: `12.3.0`
- Source: https://github.com/mermaid-js/mermaid.git @ `2086904e76c692331e34a45d7cc3bc30860d040e`
- Relationship: `behavior-reference`, `copied`, `fixtures`, `generated`, `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/mermaid/LICENSE`

## roxmltree private XML DOM

The renderer privately imports roxmltree 0.21.1 with its official iterative element parsing fix so accepted deep XHTML does not exhaust the call stack. Namespace, entity, source-range and DOM semantics are retained. The adapted tree derives from ego-tree under ISC; roxmltree offers MIT or Apache-2.0 and this distribution selects MIT.

- Version: `0.21.1 with upstream iterative element fix`
- Source: https://github.com/RazrFalcon/roxmltree @ `3f5c78dea57662b43a2f41961397b1f5ffe27166`
- Relationship: `copied`, `modified`
- License: `MIT AND ISC`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/roxmltree-dom/LICENSE-MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/roxmltree-dom/LICENSE-ISC`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/roxmltree-dom/LICENSE-APACHE`

## rough-rs roughr

roughr-merman is a modified in-tree fork of the rough-rs roughr crate.

- Version: `0.12.0`
- Source: https://github.com/orhanbalci/rough-rs.git @ `b1c2d96c944da4e74275aa09892be14e2d54445a`
- Relationship: `copied`, `modified`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/rough-rs/LICENSE`

## Rough.js

The roughr fork aligns its randomization and drawing-operation semantics with Rough.js as used by Mermaid.

- Version: `4.6.6`
- Source: https://github.com/pshihn/rough.git @ `56a2762171b1294d643501e8d14f120db6b27bd7`
- Relationship: `behavior-reference`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/roughjs/LICENSE`

## unicode-linebreak

merman-unicode-linebreak is an Apache-2.0 fork of unicode-linebreak 0.1.5, preserving its published Unicode 15.0 generated tables and extending character iteration for cooperative cancellation. UPSTREAM.json records the exact v0.1.5 Git revision and crates.io archive SHA-256 3b09c83c3c29d37506a3e260c08c03743a6bb66a9cd432c6934ab501a190571f.

- Version: `0.1.5`
- Source: https://github.com/axelf4/unicode-linebreak @ `829adeed3c07ed160c1c721b61806eb902a4c1f0`
- Relationship: `copied`, `modified`
- License: `Apache-2.0`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/unicode-linebreak/LICENSE`

## @upsetjs/venn.js

The Venn family uses a modified Rust translation of the venn.js geometry and layout kernel.

- Version: `2.0.0`
- Source: https://github.com/upsetjs/venn.js.git @ `350c835aab4a92a7570963c28f725cf9f6e5f258`
- Relationship: `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/venn-js/LICENSE`

## ZenUML Core

Merman's ZenUML grammar, model, renderer, emoji/icon data, and behavior probes follow the admitted ZenUML Core 3.50.1 source baseline.

- Version: `3.50.1`
- Source: https://github.com/mermaid-js/zenuml-core.git @ `38404ccc14243ed54ab45b804b2eb6f2ca73af36`
- Relationship: `behavior-reference`, `copied`, `modified`, `translated`
- License: `MIT`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/zenuml-core/LICENSE`
```

**merman-unicode-linebreak 0.10.0-alpha.2** (THIRD\_PARTY\_NOTICES.md):

```
# Third-Party Notices

This file records source translated, copied, generated, or embedded in `crates/merman-unicode-linebreak`.
It is generated from `docs/release/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.json`.

## unicode-linebreak

merman-unicode-linebreak is an Apache-2.0 fork of unicode-linebreak 0.1.5, preserving its published Unicode 15.0 generated tables and extending character iteration for cooperative cancellation. UPSTREAM.json records the exact v0.1.5 Git revision and crates.io archive SHA-256 3b09c83c3c29d37506a3e260c08c03743a6bb66a9cd432c6934ab501a190571f.

- Version: `0.1.5`
- Source: https://github.com/axelf4/unicode-linebreak @ `829adeed3c07ed160c1c721b61806eb902a4c1f0`
- Relationship: `copied`, `modified`
- License: `Apache-2.0`
- Legal file: `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES/unicode-linebreak/LICENSE`
```
